use snapback4_lang::compile;

#[test]
fn project_builtins_are_reserved_for_parameters_locals_and_loop_bindings() {
    for (source, name) in [
        ("query q(now: time):\n  return now\n", "now"),
        ("mutation m():\n  now = 1\n  return now\n", "now"),
        ("mutation m(commit: time):\n  return commit\n", "commit"),
        ("mutation m():\n  commit = now\n  return commit\n", "commit"),
        ("mutation m(new: text):\n  return new\n", "new"),
        ("mutation m():\n  new = 1\n  return new\n", "new"),
        ("mutation m():\n  for new in range(0, 1):\n    return new\n", "new"),
        ("query q(viewer: principal):\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
        ("mutation m():\n  viewer = 'alice'\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
        ("fn helper(viewer: principal):\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
    ] {
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        let diagnostic = bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .find(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_RESERVED")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{name}: {:?}", bound.diagnostics));
        assert!(diagnostic.message.contains(&format!("`{name}`")), "{diagnostic}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn repeated_identity_declarations_name_both_method_sets() {
    let same_file = compile(&[(
        "schema.q".into(),
        "use identity password\nuse identity guests\n".into(),
    )]);
    assert_eq!(same_file.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", same_file.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &same_file.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.code, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.line, 2);
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("password") && diagnostic.message.contains("guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );

    let across_files = compile(&[
        ("schema.q".into(), "use identity guests\n".into()),
        ("accounts.q".into(), "use identity\n".into()),
    ]);
    assert_eq!(across_files.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", across_files.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &across_files.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.file, "accounts.q");
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("guests")
            && diagnostic.message.contains("password and guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );
}

fn diagnostic(source: &str, code: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Diagnostic {
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    bound
        .diagnostics
        .iter()
        .find(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == code)
        .cloned()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing {code}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics))
}

fn replace_and_compile(source: &str, authored: &str, replacement: &str) {
    let repaired = source.replacen(authored, replacement, 1);
    assert_ne!(repaired, source, "fixture does not contain {authored:?}");
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn sql_habits_name_quarry_order_and_emit_compiling_rewrites() {
    let count = "table logs:\n  kind: text <=20\n  public 'logs'\nquery total():\n  return count logs where .kind = 'error'\n";
    let d = diagnostic(count, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("aggregates take parentheses"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(count, "count logs where .kind = 'error'", &d.rewrite);

    let ascending = "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs first 20 by .at desc\n";
    let d = diagnostic(ascending, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("first 20 by .at") && d.message.contains("ascending"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("logs last 20 by .at") && !d.rewrite.contains("desc"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(ascending, "logs first 20 by .at desc", &d.rewrite);

    let descending = "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs last 20 by .at desc\n";
    let d = diagnostic(descending, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("last 20 by .at") && d.message.contains("descending"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("logs last 20 by .at") && !d.rewrite.contains("desc"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(descending, "logs last 20 by .at desc", &d.rewrite);

    let shaped = compile(&[
        ("schema.q".into(), "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs last 20 by .at desc\n".into()),
        ("shapes.q".into(), "shape desc = { id }\n".into()),
    ]);
    assert!(shaped.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", shaped.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn cross_table_identity_names_the_wrong_reference_before_scan_advice() {
    let source = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  r = secrets[secret] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists rolls[r.id]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("`r.id` is a secrets reference"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.message.contains("rolls key position 1 expects a rolls reference"), "{d}");

    let ambiguous = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  primary: secrets\n  backup: secrets\n  unique primary\n  unique backup\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(ambiguous, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("candidate keys"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.message.contains("keyed by its own id"), "{d}");

    let later_mismatch = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  secret: secrets\n  number: int\n  unique secret, number\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret, 'bad']\n";
    let d = diagnostic(later_mismatch, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.message.contains("is a secrets reference") && !d.message.contains("key position"), "{d}");

    let wrong_composite_source = "table users:\n  public 'users'\ntable secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  secret: secrets\n  number: int\n  unique secret, number\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(user: users):\n  return rolls[user, 1]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(wrong_composite_source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("`user` is a users reference") && d.message.contains("key position 1 expects a secrets reference"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("replace `user` with a secrets reference") && !d.rewrite.contains("rolls reference"), "{d}");
}

#[test]
fn positional_type_selection_keeps_row_to_id_context_after_choosing_by_id() {
    for key in ["row", "(row if choose else row)"] {
        let source = format!(
            "table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery present(id: items, choose: bool):\n  row = items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists items[{key}]\n"
        );
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{key}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    }
    for read in ["items['']", "items[''] by byId"] {
        let source = format!("table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery present():\n  return exists {read}\n");
        let d = diagnostic(&source, "E_QUARRY_TYPE");
        assert!(d.message.contains("empty text literal cannot be an id"), "{read}: {d}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn nested_single_candidate_reads_keep_the_bind_once_path() {
    let mut key = "id".to_owned();
    // Deep enough to expose recursive duplicate binding while staying below
    // the language's existing nested-assert diagnostic expansion ceiling.
    for _ in 0..6 {
        key = format!("(items[{key}] ! else NOT_FOUND).id");
    }
    let source = format!(
        "table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery nested(id: items):\n  return items[{key}]\n"
    );
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn invalid_search_keeps_the_table_symbol_for_later_references() {
    let source = "table businesses:\n  category: int\n  search category\n  public 'businesses'\nquery one(id: businesses):\n  return businesses[id]\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("search category requires a text or enum column on businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(!bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("unknown table businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn multiline_require_rewrite_preserves_the_authored_refusal_code() {
    let source = "query choose(a: bool, b: bool):\n  require (a or\n    b)\n    else NOPE\n  return 1\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("else NOPE"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.rewrite.contains("NOT_ALLOWED"), "{d}");
    let replacement = d.rewrite.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2)
        .find(|part| part.starts_with("require ")).expect("the one-line require repair");
    replace_and_compile(source, "require (a or\n    b)\n    else NOPE", replacement);
}

#[test]
fn reviewed_disclosure_silences_only_the_maintain_delta_note() {
    let source = "table events:\n  owner: principal\n  category: text <=20\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- allow\nmaintain totals: count events by category\n  read <- allow\n";
    let warned = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(warned.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", warned.notes);
    let reviewed = compile(&[(
        "schema.q".into(),
        source.replace("  read <- allow\n", "  read <- allow\n  disclosure reviewed 'product staff intentionally see category totals'\n"),
    )]);
    assert!(reviewed.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", reviewed.diagnostics);
    assert!(reviewed.notes.iter().all(|note| note.code != "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", reviewed.notes);

    for line in [
        "  disclosure reviewed ''\n",
        "  disclosure reviewed 'first review'\n  disclosure reviewed 'second review'\n",
    ] {
        let rejected = compile(&[(
            "schema.q".into(),
            source.replace("  read <- allow\n", &format!("  read <- allow\n{line}")),
        )]);
        assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX"), "{:#?}", rejected.diagnostics);
    }
}

#[test]
fn structural_fanout_leads_with_a_direct_viewer_predicate() {
    let source = "table bottles:\n  organizer: principal\n  read <- .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\ntable shares:\n  bottle: bottles\n  member: principal\n  organizer: principal\n  cap 120 by bottle\n  read <- .member = viewer or .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\nmutation cancel(bottle: bottles):\n  b = bottles[bottle] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require b.organizer = viewer else NOT_ORGANIZER\n  for share in shares where .bottle = bottle:\n    update shares[share.id] { member: share.member }\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("First add `.member = viewer`"), "{d}");
}

#[test]
fn unreadable_probe_names_an_existing_require_read_path() {
    let source = "table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes):\n  row = notes[note] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require row.author = viewer else NOT_AUTHOR\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
    assert!(note.message.contains("require/read path already established a readable notes row"), "{note}");

    for guard in [
        "require not exists notes[note] else PRESENT",
        "require exists notes[note] or other else NOT_ALLOWED",
    ] {
        let source = format!("table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes, other: bool):\n  {guard}\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n");
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{guard}: {:#?}", bound.notes));
        assert!(!note.message.contains("require/read path already established"), "{guard}: {note}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn delete_restrict_note_opens_the_matching_guide_section() {
    let source = "table parents:\n  read <- allow\n  delete <- allow\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  read <- allow\nmutation drop(parent: parents):\n  delete parents[parent]\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED")
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
    assert!(note.rewrite.contains("guide writes --section \"On delete restrict\""), "{note}");
    assert!(snapback4_lang::guide::card("writes").unwrap().contains("## On delete restrict"));
}
//! Expression lowering with branch-owned setup statements.

use super::*;
use snapback4_core::calendar::Civil;

impl Binder<'_> {
    /// Bare tables are domains only at an aggregate/loop consumer; ordinary
    /// names keep their lexical meaning and ordinary pages keep their bounds.
    pub(super) fn aggregate_domain(&self, value: &A) -> Option<ReadExpr> {
        match value {
            A::Read(read) if read.range.is_none() && read.matching.is_none()
                && !self.catalog.tables.get(&read.table).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) => Some(read.clone()),
            A::Name { name, site } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name)
                && !self.catalog.tables.get(name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) =>
                Some(ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, order_keys: Vec::new(), computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), index: Some("byId".into()), range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() }),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    // ----- expressions -----

    /// An expression whose value is used (bound, returned, or a final
    /// write): writes yield their row.
    pub(super) fn value_of(&mut self, value: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        if let A::Write { .. } = value {
            return self.write(value, out, true).map(|opt| opt.expect("a valued write"));
        }
        self.expr(value, out)
    }

    /// Strict compound children run in source order, before setup emitted by
    /// a later child. Lexical values and their fields cannot change here;
    /// leaving those fields in their object also preserves pending provenance.
    pub(super) fn snapshot(&mut self, value: Expr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Expr {
        if matches!(value, Expr::Literal(_) | Expr::Input(Input::Viewer | Input::Now)) || lexical_value(&value) {
            return value;
        }
        let name = self.temp("value");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: name.clone(), value });
        ir::var(&name)
    }

    pub(super) fn sequence_setup<'e>(&mut self, prior: impl Iterator<Item = &'e mut Expr>, setup: setup::Body, out: &mut setup::Body) {
        if !setup.is_empty() {
            for value in prior {
                *value = self.snapshot(std::mem::replace(value, Expr::Literal(Value::Null)), out);
            }
            out.extend(setup);
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn strict_args(&mut self, args: &[A], out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Vec<(Expr, Ty)>> {
        let mut values = Vec::<(Expr, Ty)>::new();
        let mut failed = None;
        for arg in args {
            let mut setup = setup::Body::default();
            match self.expr(arg, &mut setup) {
                Ok(value) => {
                    self.sequence_setup(values.iter_mut().map(|(e, _)| e), setup, out);
                    values.push(value);
                }
                Err(error) => { self.record_error(error.clone()); failed.get_or_insert(error); }
            }
        }
        match failed { Some(error) => Err(error), None => Ok(values) }
    }

    pub(super) fn expr(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        // Only shape/object/list construction forwards output position. Every
        // operator, call, key, predicate and assertion consumes its operands.
        let prior = self.returned_data;
        self.returned_data &= matches!(expr, A::Array { .. } | A::Object { .. } | A::Shape { .. } | A::ShapeUse { .. } | A::Member { .. } | A::Name { .. } | A::Focal { .. });
        let start = out.len();
        let result = self.expr_inner(expr, out);
        if !matches!(expr, A::Write { .. }) && point_facts::writes(&out[start..]) { self.invalidate_points(); }
        self.returned_data = prior;
        // Even a builtin's ordinary type refusal should explain why a whole
        // maintained value cannot be consumed. Successful calls are checked by
        // the shared IR allowlist; this only improves already-failing calls.
        if result.as_ref().is_err_and(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_TYPE") {
            let args: Vec<&A> = match expr { A::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().collect(), A::Binary { left, right, .. } => vec![left, right], _ => vec![] };
            let mut probe = self.clone();
            for arg in args {
                if let Ok((_, ty)) = probe.expr(arg, &mut setup::Body::default()) {
                    self.maintenance_operand(&ty, arg.site())?;
                }
            }
        }
        let result = result?;
        if !self.schema.receipts.is_empty() {
            let site = self.call_chain.iter().find(|(name, _)| self.catalog.documents.iter().flat_map(|d| &d.tables).filter_map(|t| t.receipts.as_ref()).any(|clause| &clause.operations[0] == name))
                .map(|(_, site)| site).unwrap_or_else(|| expr.site());
            self.receipt_sources.push((result.0.clone(), site.clone()));
        }
        Ok(result)
    }

    fn maintenance_operand(&self, ty: &Ty, site: &Site) -> Fallible<()> {
        match ty {
            Ty::Row(table) | Ty::WriteRow(table) | Ty::Page(table, _) => {
                if self.schema.row_view_predicate_field(table, "recountPending").is_some() {
                    return Err(Diagnostic::new(site, "E_VIEW_KEY", format!("value contains {table}.recountPending/pending maintenance state"),
                        "project the field you need first (value, member identity or copied member fields); return maintenance state only as query data").guide("maintains"));
                }
            },
            Ty::Optional(item) | Ty::List(item, _) | Ty::Collection(item, _) => self.maintenance_operand(item, site)?,
            Ty::Object(fields) | Ty::RowShape(fields) | Ty::RowObject(fields) => for ty in fields.values() { self.maintenance_operand(ty, site)?; },
            _ => {},
        }
        Ok(())
    }

    pub(super) fn computation(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.returned_data, false);
        let result = self.expr(expr, out);
        self.returned_data = prior;
        result
    }

    fn expr_inner(&mut self, expr: &A, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let site = expr.site();
        match expr {
            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.view_reads.contains_key(name) => {
                let mut merge = self.catalog.view_reads[name].clone();
                merge.site = site.clone();
                self.planned_view(&merge, out, prefix::Position::Page)
            }
            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none()
                && self.catalog.tables.get(name).and_then(|table| table.maintained.as_ref()).is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) => {
                // @ref LLP 3000.013.001#3-global-maintained-view-representation — an ungrouped view name is its scalar, not a table page.
                self.read(&ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None, index: Some("byGroup".into()), order_keys: Vec::new(), range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone() }, out)
            }
            A::Name { name, .. } if self.binding(name).is_none() && self.catalog.tables.contains_key(name) => {
                self.read(&ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), table: name.clone(), keys: Vec::new(), seeks: Vec::new(), filter: None, index: Some("byId".into()),
                    order_keys: vec!["id".into()], range: None, matching: None, site: site.clone() }, out)
            }
            A::Name { name, .. } => self.name(name, site),
            A::Focal { .. } => match &self.focal {
                Some((var, ty)) => Ok((ir::var(var), ty.clone())),
                None => Err(unknown(site, "focal . is valid only inside a row shape", "name the row: `row.field`")),
            },
            A::Literal { value, .. } => Ok(match value {
                Literal::Str(text) => (Expr::Literal(Value::String(text.clone())), Ty::Text(text.chars().count() as u32)),
                Literal::Number(text) => (Expr::Literal(Value::Int(super::super::integer_literal(text, site)?)), Ty::Int),
                Literal::Bool(b) => (Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(*b)), Ty::Bool),
                Literal::Null => (Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null),
            }),
            A::Array { items, .. } => {
                let mut exprs = Vec::new();
                let mut item_ty: Option<Ty> = None;
                let args = self.strict_args(items, out)?;
                let origins: Vec<_> = args.iter().zip(items).map(|((_, ty), source)| (ty.clone(), source.clone())).collect();
                for (e, t) in args {
                    item_ty = Some(match item_ty {
                        Some(j) => j.join_item(&t).map_err(|error| self.join_error_many(error.at("[]"), &origins))?,
                        None => t,
                    });
                    exprs.push(e);
                }
                let max = exprs.len() as u32;
                Ok((Expr::Array(exprs), Ty::List(Box::new(item_ty.unwrap_or(Ty::Unknown)), max)))
            }
            A::Object { fields, .. } => self.object(fields, out),
            A::Unary { op, value, .. } => {
                let (e, t) = self.expr(value, out)?;
                if op == "not" {
                    if !t.is_bool() {
                        let operand = print::expression(value);
                        return Err(type_error(site, format!("not needs a bool, got {} — `not` binds tighter than a comparison, so `not {operand} = x` reads as `(not {operand}) = x`", t.name()),
                            format!("write `{operand} != x`, or parenthesize: `not ({operand} = x)`")));
                    }
                    Ok((Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Not, value: Box::new(e) }, Ty::Bool))
                } else {
                    if !t.is_numeric() {
                        return Err(type_error(site, format!("unary - needs a present number, got {}", t.name()), "negate an int, decimal or money value"));
                    }
                    Ok((Expr::Unary { op: UnaryOp::Neg, value: Box::new(e) }, t))
                }
            }
            A::Binary { op, left, right, .. } => self.binary(op, left, right, site, out),
            A::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => self.conditional(then, condition, otherwise, out),
            A::Member { base, name, .. } => {
                // Generated maintain keys are literal path-named columns. Resolve
                // the full chain before ordinary member/reference traversal.
                if let Some((root, path)) = aggregate_path(expr) {
                    let (e, t) = self.computation(root, out)?;
                    if let Ty::Row(table) = t.required()
                        && let Some(info) = self.catalog.tables.get(&table)
                        && info.maintained.is_some() && info.columns.contains_key(&path)
                    {
                        let (value, ty) = self.member(e, t, &path, site)?;
                        return Ok((value, self.field_refinement(expr).unwrap_or(ty)));
                    }
                }
                let (e, t) = self.computation(base, out)?;
                if name == "length" && matches!(t.required(), Ty::Text(_) | Ty::List(..) | Ty::Page(..) | Ty::Collection(..)) {
                    return Err(unsupported(site, ".length", format!("count({})", print::expression(base))));
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — a required reference's field reads its row:
                // `x.ref.col` is `t[x.ref].col`, the same authorized point read
                // (null when the row is missing or hidden), bound once.
                // A recorded attribution column spelled `ref.col` names the value
                // copied at insert; the live read would be a second meaning.
                let recorded = matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Member { name: column, .. }
                    if self.catalog.tables.values().any(|info| info.recorded.contains_key(&format!("{column}.{name}"))));
                if let Ty::Ref(table) = &t && name != "id" && !recorded
                    && self.catalog.tables.get(table).is_some_and(|info| info.maintained.is_none() && info.decl.ephemeral.is_none())
                {
                    let local = self.temp("reference");
                    let runtime = self.declare(&local, t.clone());
                    out.push(Stmt::Const { name: runtime, value: e });
                    let key = ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Name { name: local, site: site.clone() }, site: site.clone() };
                    let read = ReadExpr { cursor_prefix: None, computed: Default::default(), order_keys: Vec::new(), table: table.clone(), keys: vec![key], index: None, range: None, matching: None, filter: None, seeks: Vec::new(), site: site.clone() };
                    return self.computation(&A::Member { base: Box::new(A::Read(read)), name: name.clone(), site: site.clone() }, out);
                }
                // @ref LLP 3000.036#13.7 — `x.ref.col` reads one reference. A second hop
                // (`r.planting.bed.owner`) gets the two steps that compile, by name.
                if let Ty::Ref(target) = t.required() && t.is_optional() && name != "id"
                    && let Some(rewrite) = self.second_hop_rewrite(base, &target, name) {
                    return Err(type_error(site, format!("`{}` reads two references; `x.ref.col` reads one ({} may be missing or hidden)", print::expression(expr), print::expression(base)), rewrite).guide("shapes"));
                }
                let (value, ty) = self.member(e, t, name, site)?;
                Ok((value, self.field_refinement(expr).unwrap_or(ty)))
            }
            A::Call { name, args, .. } => {
                let (value, ty) = self.call(name, args, site, out)?;
                self.record_call(expr, &ty);
                Ok((value, self.call_refinement(expr).unwrap_or(ty)))
            },
            A::Read(read) if read.table == "connected" && !self.catalog.tables.contains_key("connected") => self.connected(read, out),
            A::Read(read) => {
                let (value, ty) = self.read(read, out)?;
                Ok((value, if self.point_present(read) { ty.required() } else { ty }))
            },
            A::Merge(merge) => self.merge(merge, out),
            A::Exists { read, .. } if read.table == "connected" && !self.catalog.tables.contains_key("connected") => {
                let (count, _) = self.connected(read, out)?;
                Ok((Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Gt, left: Box::new(count), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Int(0))) }, Ty::Bool))
            }
            A::Exists { read, .. } => {
                self.exists_read(read, out)
            }
            A::Created { .. } => Err(unknown(site, "created is valid only in an insert table rule", "use exists here")),
            A::Assert { value, code, message, .. } => {
                validate_code(site, code)?;
                let (mut e, t) = self.expr(value, out)?;
                // A row point may return null because its read meter cut. Do
                // not turn that unavailable fact into an authored NOT_FOUND.
                if self.caller_kind == ProgramKind::Query && t.is_optional() && matches!(t.required(), Ty::Row(_)) {
                    e = Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::RequireUncapped, args: vec![e] };
                }
                let tmp = self.temp("present");
                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: tmp.clone(), value: e });
                out.push(Stmt::Require { condition: Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&tmp)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) }, code: code.clone(), site: Some(site.to_string()), message: message.clone() });
                if let A::Read(read) = value.as_ref() { self.narrow_point(read); }
                Ok((ir::var(&tmp), t.required()))
            }
            A::Shape { base, fields, .. } => self.shape(base, fields, site, out),
            A::ShapeUse { base, name, .. } => {
                let Some(shape) = self.catalog.shapes.get(name).cloned() else {
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036.002#9 — diagnose SQL's trailing desc only after project-wide shape resolution.
                    if name == "desc" && let A::Read(read) = base.as_ref() && let Some(range) = &read.range {
                        let page = format!(
                            "{} {} by {}",
                            if range.order == ast::Order::Desc { "last" } else { "first" },
                            range.take,
                            if read.order_keys.is_empty() {
                                read.index.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| "<index>".into())
                            } else {
                                read.order_keys.iter().map(|key| format!(".{key}")).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")
                            },
                        );
                        let mut repaired = read.clone();
                        repaired.range.as_mut().expect("the diagnosed page has a range").order = ast::Order::Desc;
                        return Err(Diagnostic::new(
                            site,
                            "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX",
                            format!("`{page}` is already {}; Quarry has no trailing `desc`", if range.order == ast::Order::Desc { "descending" } else { "ascending" }),
                            print::read_expr(&repaired),
                        )
                        .guide("from-sql"));
                    }
                    return Err(unknown(site, format!("unknown shape {name}"), name_rewrite(name, self.catalog.shapes.keys().map(String::as_str), Some("declare `shape name = { fields }`"))));
                };
                self.shape(base, &shape.fields, site, out)
            }
            A::Walk { table, seed, column, depth, .. } => {
                let (recurse, ty) = self.walk(table, seed, column, *depth, site, out)?;
                let list = self.temp("walk");
                out.push(Stmt::Let { name: list.clone(), value: Expr::Array(Vec::new()) });
                let item = self.temp("node");
                out.push(Stmt::For { binding: item.clone(), domain: LoopDomain::Recurse(recurse), body: vec![Stmt::Push { target: list.clone(), value: ir::var(&item) }] });
                Ok((ir::var(&list), Ty::List(Box::new(ty), depth.saturating_add(1))))
            }
            A::Write { .. } => self.write(expr, out, true).map(|opt| opt.expect("a valued write")),
        }
    }

    /// `connected[p]`: how many connections the principal holds open on the
    /// server now, an int; `exists connected[p]` is the bool. Not a table:
    /// the server's registry of long polls answers it, a device answers 1
    /// and marks its read, and the server decides the write.
    fn connected(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        let site = &read.site;
        if read.keys.len() != 1 || read.range.is_some() || read.matching.is_some() || read.index.is_some() {
            return Err(unknown(site, "connected takes one principal: connected[p] counts its connections, exists connected[p] tests", "exists connected[m.principal]"));
        }
        let key = &read.keys[0];
        let (e, t) = self.expr_as_identity(&key.value, out, &Ty::Principal)?;
        if !matches!(t.required(), Ty::Principal | Ty::Id) {
            return Err(type_error(key.value.site(), format!("connected takes a principal, got {}", t.name()), "connected[m.principal]"));
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.1 — retain the author's deciding read alongside the IR, never in its serialized form.
        self.presence_sources.push((e.clone(), site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        self.sources.insert(format!("presence/{site}"), (site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        Ok((Expr::Builtin { builtin: Builtin::Connected, args: vec![e] }, Ty::Int))
    }

    pub(super) fn name(&mut self, name: &str, site: &Site) -> Fallible<(Expr, Ty)> {
        match name {
            "viewer" | "$input:viewer" => Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Viewer), Ty::Principal)),
            "now" | "commit" => {
                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query && name == "commit" {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "commit is the mutation commit clock; queries read now", "now"));
                }
                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::Now), Ty::Time))
            }
            "new" => {
                if self.viewer_depth.is_some() { return Err(job_reads::read_only(site)); }
                if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query {
                    return Err(unknown(site, "queries cannot mint ids", "mint ids in a mutation"));
                }
                Ok((Expr::Input(Input::NewId), Ty::Id))
            }
            "true" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(true)), Ty::Bool)),
            "false" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Bool(false)), Ty::Bool)),
            "null" => Ok((Expr::Literal(Value::Null), Ty::Null)),
            _ => {
                if let Some(binding) = self.binding(name) {
                    if let Some(error) = &binding.error { return Err(error.clone()); }

exec
/bin/bash -lc "rg -n '"'^## 9|''^## |''^### 9'"' llp/3000.036.002-intent-aware-lints-and-bootstrap.rfc.md
sed -n '1,255p' snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/tests/wave12_diagnostics.rs
rg -n 'ReadExpr|Range|struct KeyPart' snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/ast.rs
rg -n 'read_expr|fn keys|fn range|cursor_prefix|computed' snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/print.rs
rg -n 'desc|ShapeUse|order_keys|cursor_prefix|computed|fn read|range' snapback4/crates/snapback4-lang/src/parser/expr.rs" in /home/ccheever/projects/sb4-w12-lang
 succeeded in 0ms:
11:## 1. Scope and motivation
20:## 2. Service seats grant membership on the server
48:## 3. Readiness notes identify blockers
61:## 4. Fixture intent and guard visibility
132:## 5. Refusals and usable rewrites
169:## 6. Acceptance and review
180:## 7. Wave 11 diagnostic precision (QDIAG)
210:## 8. Wave 11 contextual spellings and refusal codes (QDIAG amendment)
287:## 9. Wave 12 declaration safety and diagnostic fidelity
use snapback4_lang::compile;

#[test]
fn project_builtins_are_reserved_for_parameters_locals_and_loop_bindings() {
    for (source, name) in [
        ("query q(now: time):\n  return now\n", "now"),
        ("mutation m():\n  now = 1\n  return now\n", "now"),
        ("mutation m(commit: time):\n  return commit\n", "commit"),
        ("mutation m():\n  commit = now\n  return commit\n", "commit"),
        ("mutation m(new: text):\n  return new\n", "new"),
        ("mutation m():\n  new = 1\n  return new\n", "new"),
        ("mutation m():\n  for new in range(0, 1):\n    return new\n", "new"),
        ("query q(viewer: principal):\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
        ("mutation m():\n  viewer = 'alice'\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
        ("fn helper(viewer: principal):\n  return viewer\n", "viewer"),
    ] {
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
        let diagnostic = bound
            .diagnostics
            .iter()
            .find(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_RESERVED")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{name}: {:?}", bound.diagnostics));
        assert!(diagnostic.message.contains(&format!("`{name}`")), "{diagnostic}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn repeated_identity_declarations_name_both_method_sets() {
    let same_file = compile(&[(
        "schema.q".into(),
        "use identity password\nuse identity guests\n".into(),
    )]);
    assert_eq!(same_file.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", same_file.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &same_file.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.code, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.line, 2);
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("password") && diagnostic.message.contains("guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );

    let across_files = compile(&[
        ("schema.q".into(), "use identity guests\n".into()),
        ("accounts.q".into(), "use identity\n".into()),
    ]);
    assert_eq!(across_files.diagnostics.len(), 1, "{:?}", across_files.diagnostics);
    let diagnostic = &across_files.diagnostics[0];
    assert_eq!(diagnostic.site.file, "accounts.q");
    assert!(
        diagnostic.message.contains("guests")
            && diagnostic.message.contains("password and guests"),
        "{diagnostic}"
    );
}

fn diagnostic(source: &str, code: &str) -> snapback4_lang::Diagnostic {
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    bound
        .diagnostics
        .iter()
        .find(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == code)
        .cloned()
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("missing {code}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics))
}

fn replace_and_compile(source: &str, authored: &str, replacement: &str) {
    let repaired = source.replacen(authored, replacement, 1);
    assert_ne!(repaired, source, "fixture does not contain {authored:?}");
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), repaired)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn sql_habits_name_quarry_order_and_emit_compiling_rewrites() {
    let count = "table logs:\n  kind: text <=20\n  public 'logs'\nquery total():\n  return count logs where .kind = 'error'\n";
    let d = diagnostic(count, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("aggregates take parentheses"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(count, "count logs where .kind = 'error'", &d.rewrite);

    let ascending = "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs first 20 by .at desc\n";
    let d = diagnostic(ascending, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("first 20 by .at") && d.message.contains("ascending"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("logs last 20 by .at") && !d.rewrite.contains("desc"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(ascending, "logs first 20 by .at desc", &d.rewrite);

    let descending = "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs last 20 by .at desc\n";
    let d = diagnostic(descending, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.message.contains("last 20 by .at") && d.message.contains("descending"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("logs last 20 by .at") && !d.rewrite.contains("desc"), "{d}");
    replace_and_compile(descending, "logs last 20 by .at desc", &d.rewrite);

    let shaped = compile(&[
        ("schema.q".into(), "table logs:\n  at: time\n  public 'logs'\nquery recent():\n  return logs last 20 by .at desc\n".into()),
        ("shapes.q".into(), "shape desc = { id }\n".into()),
    ]);
    assert!(shaped.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", shaped.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn cross_table_identity_names_the_wrong_reference_before_scan_advice() {
    let source = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  r = secrets[secret] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists rolls[r.id]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("`r.id` is a secrets reference"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.message.contains("rolls key position 1 expects a rolls reference"), "{d}");

    let ambiguous = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  primary: secrets\n  backup: secrets\n  unique primary\n  unique backup\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(ambiguous, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("candidate keys"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.message.contains("keyed by its own id"), "{d}");

    let later_mismatch = "table secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  secret: secrets\n  number: int\n  unique secret, number\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(secret: secrets):\n  return rolls[secret, 'bad']\n";
    let d = diagnostic(later_mismatch, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("no declared unique key accepting these positional argument types"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.message.contains("is a secrets reference") && !d.message.contains("key position"), "{d}");

    let wrong_composite_source = "table users:\n  public 'users'\ntable secrets:\n  public 'secrets'\ntable rolls:\n  secret: secrets\n  number: int\n  unique secret, number\n  public 'rolls'\nquery wrong(user: users):\n  return rolls[user, 1]\n";
    let d = diagnostic(wrong_composite_source, "E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN");
    assert!(d.message.contains("`user` is a users reference") && d.message.contains("key position 1 expects a secrets reference"), "{d}");
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("replace `user` with a secrets reference") && !d.rewrite.contains("rolls reference"), "{d}");
}

#[test]
fn positional_type_selection_keeps_row_to_id_context_after_choosing_by_id() {
    for key in ["row", "(row if choose else row)"] {
        let source = format!(
            "table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery present(id: items, choose: bool):\n  row = items[id] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  return exists items[{key}]\n"
        );
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{key}: {:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    }
    for read in ["items['']", "items[''] by byId"] {
        let source = format!("table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery present():\n  return exists {read}\n");
        let d = diagnostic(&source, "E_QUARRY_TYPE");
        assert!(d.message.contains("empty text literal cannot be an id"), "{read}: {d}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn nested_single_candidate_reads_keep_the_bind_once_path() {
    let mut key = "id".to_owned();
    // Deep enough to expose recursive duplicate binding while staying below
    // the language's existing nested-assert diagnostic expansion ceiling.
    for _ in 0..6 {
        key = format!("(items[{key}] ! else NOT_FOUND).id");
    }
    let source = format!(
        "table items:\n  public 'items'\nquery nested(id: items):\n  return items[{key}]\n"
    );
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn invalid_search_keeps_the_table_symbol_for_later_references() {
    let source = "table businesses:\n  category: int\n  search category\n  public 'businesses'\nquery one(id: businesses):\n  return businesses[id]\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("search category requires a text or enum column on businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
    assert!(!bound.diagnostics.iter().any(|d| d.message.contains("unknown table businesses")), "{:#?}", bound.diagnostics);
}

#[test]
fn multiline_require_rewrite_preserves_the_authored_refusal_code() {
    let source = "query choose(a: bool, b: bool):\n  require (a or\n    b)\n    else NOPE\n  return 1\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX");
    assert!(d.rewrite.contains("else NOPE"), "{d}");
    assert!(!d.rewrite.contains("NOT_ALLOWED"), "{d}");
    let replacement = d.rewrite.split('`').skip(1).step_by(2)
        .find(|part| part.starts_with("require ")).expect("the one-line require repair");
    replace_and_compile(source, "require (a or\n    b)\n    else NOPE", replacement);
}

#[test]
fn reviewed_disclosure_silences_only_the_maintain_delta_note() {
    let source = "table events:\n  owner: principal\n  category: text <=20\n  read <- .owner = viewer\n  insert <- .owner = viewer\n  update <- allow\nmaintain totals: count events by category\n  read <- allow\n";
    let warned = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    assert!(warned.notes.iter().any(|note| note.code == "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", warned.notes);
    let reviewed = compile(&[(
        "schema.q".into(),
        source.replace("  read <- allow\n", "  read <- allow\n  disclosure reviewed 'product staff intentionally see category totals'\n"),
    )]);
    assert!(reviewed.diagnostics.is_empty(), "{:#?}", reviewed.diagnostics);
    assert!(reviewed.notes.iter().all(|note| note.code != "N_MAINTAIN_DELTA"), "{:#?}", reviewed.notes);

    for line in [
        "  disclosure reviewed ''\n",
        "  disclosure reviewed 'first review'\n  disclosure reviewed 'second review'\n",
    ] {
        let rejected = compile(&[(
            "schema.q".into(),
            source.replace("  read <- allow\n", &format!("  read <- allow\n{line}")),
        )]);
        assert!(rejected.diagnostics.iter().any(|diagnostic| diagnostic.code == "E_QUARRY_SYNTAX"), "{:#?}", rejected.diagnostics);
    }
}

#[test]
fn structural_fanout_leads_with_a_direct_viewer_predicate() {
    let source = "table bottles:\n  organizer: principal\n  read <- .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\ntable shares:\n  bottle: bottles\n  member: principal\n  organizer: principal\n  cap 120 by bottle\n  read <- .member = viewer or .organizer = viewer\n  update <- .organizer = viewer\nmutation cancel(bottle: bottles):\n  b = bottles[bottle] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require b.organizer = viewer else NOT_ORGANIZER\n  for share in shares where .bottle = bottle:\n    update shares[share.id] { member: share.member }\n";
    let d = diagnostic(source, "E_STRUCTURAL_FANOUT");
    assert!(d.rewrite.starts_with("First add `.member = viewer`"), "{d}");
}

#[test]
fn unreadable_probe_names_an_existing_require_read_path() {
    let source = "table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes):\n  row = notes[note] ! else NOT_FOUND\n  require row.author = viewer else NOT_AUTHOR\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
    assert!(note.message.contains("require/read path already established a readable notes row"), "{note}");

    for guard in [
        "require not exists notes[note] else PRESENT",
        "require exists notes[note] or other else NOT_ALLOWED",
    ] {
        let source = format!("table notes:\n  author: principal\n  read <- .author = viewer\ntable gatecodes:\n  note: notes\n  unique note\n  read <- exists notes[.note, author = viewer]\nmutation setCode(note: notes, other: bool):\n  {guard}\n  if exists gatecodes[note]:\n    return 1\n  return 0\n");
        let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source)]);
        let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_UNREADABLE_PROBE")
            .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{guard}: {:#?}", bound.notes));
        assert!(!note.message.contains("require/read path already established"), "{guard}: {note}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn delete_restrict_note_opens_the_matching_guide_section() {
    let source = "table parents:\n  read <- allow\n  delete <- allow\ntable children:\n  parent: parents\n  read <- allow\nmutation drop(parent: parents):\n  delete parents[parent]\n";
    let bound = compile(&[("schema.q".into(), source.into())]);
    let note = bound.notes.iter().find(|note| note.code == "N_DELETE_RESTRICTED")
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{:#?}", bound.notes));
    assert!(note.rewrite.contains("guide writes --section \"On delete restrict\""), "{note}");
    assert!(snapback4_lang::guide::card("writes").unwrap().contains("## On delete restrict"));
}
