//! Diagnostic-only source edits. A malformed span must lose its repair, never
//! crash the compiler or splice a different declaration.
use super::*;
use crate::types::Ty;
use std::ops::Range;

impl Parser {
    pub(super) fn table_decl(&self, name: &str) -> Option<&TableDecl> {
        self.document.tables.iter().chain(&self.known_tables).find(|table| table.name == name)
    }

    pub(super) fn column_type(&self, table: &str, column: &str) -> Option<ScalarType> {
        if let Some(table) = self.table_decl(table) {
            return if column == "id" { Some(ScalarType { kind: TypeKind::Id, optional: false }) }
                else { table.columns.iter().find(|c| c.name == column).map(|c| c.type_.clone()) };
        }
        let Ty::RowShape(fields) = self.known_views.get(table)? else { return None; };
        scalar_type(fields.get(column)?)
    }

    fn source_offset(&self, site: &Site) -> Option<usize> {
        let line = usize::try_from(site.line).ok()?.checked_sub(1)?;
        let col = usize::try_from(site.col).ok()?.checked_sub(1)?;
        let text = self.source.get(line)?;
        let byte = text.char_indices().map(|(i, _)| i).chain([text.len()]).nth(col)?;
        Some(self.source.get(..line)?.iter().map(|s| s.len() + 1).sum::<usize>() + byte)
    }

    fn token_end(&self, token: &Token, source: &str) -> Option<usize> {
        let start = self.source_offset(&token.site)?;
        let tail = source.get(start..)?;
        if token.kind != Kind::Str {
            return tail.starts_with(&token.value).then(|| start + token.value.len());
        }
        let mut chars = tail.char_indices();
        let (_, quote @ ('\'' | '"')) = chars.next()? else { return None; };
        while let Some((i, ch)) = chars.next() {
            if ch == '\\' { chars.next()?; }
            else if ch == quote { return Some(start + i + ch.len_utf8()); }
        }
        None
    }

    // Delimiters suppress layout tokens. A closing ')' at column one therefore
    // stays in the declaration, regardless of its physical indentation.
    pub(super) fn declaration_tokens(&self) -> Option<Range<usize>> {
        let mut indent = 0usize;
        let mut delimiters = Vec::new();
        let mut statement_start = false;
        for (i, token) in self.tokens.iter().enumerate().skip(self.declaration_start) {
            match token.kind {
                Kind::Eof => return delimiters.is_empty().then_some(self.declaration_start..i),
                Kind::Indent => { indent += 1; continue; }
                Kind::Dedent => { indent = indent.checked_sub(1)?; statement_start = true; continue; }
                Kind::Newline => { statement_start = true; continue; }
                _ => {}
            }
            if statement_start && indent == 0 && delimiters.is_empty() {
                return Some(self.declaration_start..i);
            }
            statement_start = false;
            if token.kind == Kind::Symbol {
                match token.value.as_str() {
                    "(" => delimiters.push(")"), "[" => delimiters.push("]"), "{" => delimiters.push("}"),
                    ")" | "]" | "}" if delimiters.pop() != Some(token.value.as_str()) => return None,
                    _ => {}
                }
            }
        }
        None
    }

    pub(super) fn fresh_parameter(&self, column: &str, args: &[ArgDecl]) -> Option<String> {
        let tokens = self.tokens.get(self.declaration_tokens()?)?;
        let bindings: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = tokens.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(i, token)| {
            (token.kind == Kind::Word && tokens.get(i + 1).is_some_and(|t| t.value == "=")
                && i.checked_sub(1).and_then(|j| tokens.get(j)).is_none_or(|t| t.value != "."))
                .then_some(token.value.as_str())
        }).collect();
        let mut name = column.to_owned();
        while bindings.contains(name.as_str()) || args.iter().any(|arg| arg.name == name) { name.push_str("Argument"); }
        Some(name)
    }

    fn token_span(&self, tokens: Range<usize>) -> Option<Range<usize>> {
        if tokens.start >= tokens.end { return None; }
        let source = self.source.join("\n");
        let start = self.source_offset(&self.tokens.get(tokens.start)?.site)?;
        let end = self.token_end(self.tokens.get(tokens.end.checked_sub(1)?)?, &source)?;
        source.get(start..end)?;
        Some(start..end)
    }

    fn comparison_span(&self, site: &Site) -> Option<Range<usize>> {
        self.token_span(self.comparison_spans.get(&(site.line, site.col))?.clone())
    }

    pub(super) fn comparison_source(&self, site: &Site) -> Option<String> {
        self.source.join("\n").get(self.comparison_span(site)?).map(str::to_owned)
    }

    pub(super) fn predicate_rewrite(&mut self, start: usize, end: usize, site: &Site, replacement: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let span = self.token_span(start.checked_sub(1)?..end)?;
        let rewrite = self.source_rewrite(span.clone(), vec![(self.comparison_span(site)?, replacement.into())])?;
        self.register_bytes(site, span, &rewrite, None)?;
        Some(rewrite.strip_prefix("where ")?.into())
    }

    pub(super) fn declaration_rewrite(&mut self, site: &Site, replacement: &str, argument: Option<(&Site, String)>) -> Option<String> {
        let source = self.source.join("\n");
        let tokens = self.declaration_tokens()?;
        let start = self.source_offset(&self.tokens.get(tokens.start)?.site)?;
        let last = self.tokens.get(tokens.end)?;
        let end = if last.kind == Kind::Eof { source.len() } else { self.source_offset(&last.site)? };
        let comparison = self.comparison_span(site)?;
        let mut edits = vec![(comparison.clone(), replacement.into())];
        if let Some((site, value)) = argument {
            let at = self.source_offset(site)?;
            if source.get(at..)?.chars().next()? != '(' || at >= comparison.start { return None; }
            let at = at.checked_add(1)?;
            edits.push((at..at, value));
        }
        let rewrite = self.source_rewrite(start..end, edits)?;
        self.register_bytes(site, start..end, &rewrite, None)?;
        Some(rewrite)
    }

    // Edit only the bad conjunct and the key insertion site. Every retained
    // operand keeps its authored scope, comments, grouping and modifiers.
    pub(super) fn keyed_read_rewrite(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, start: usize,
        predicates: &[(Range<usize>, Expr)], site: &Site, column: &str) -> Option<String> {
        let table = (start..self.position).find(|&i| self.tokens[i].kind == Kind::Word && self.tokens[i].value == read.table)?;
        let span = self.token_span(start..self.position)?;
        let key = format!("{column} = .{column}");
        let (at, insertion) = if self.tokens.get(table + 1)?.value == "[" {
            // Seek sites come from the parser, so operators inside key operands
            // cannot be mistaken for top-level seeks.
            if let Some(seek) = read.seeks.first() {
                (self.source_offset(&seek.site)?, format!("{key}, "))
            } else {
                let mut depth = 0usize;
                let mut close = None;
                for i in table + 1..self.position {
                    let token = self.tokens.get(i)?;
                    if token.kind != Kind::Symbol { continue; }
                    match token.value.as_str() {
                        "[" => depth += 1,
                        "]" => { depth = depth.checked_sub(1)?; if depth == 0 { close = Some(i); break; } }
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
                let close = close?;
                let separator = if matches!(self.tokens.get(close.checked_sub(1)?)?.value.as_str(), "[" | ",") { "" } else { ", " };
                (self.source_offset(&self.tokens.get(close)?.site)?, format!("{separator}{key}"))
            }
        } else {
            (self.token_end(self.tokens.get(table)?, &self.source.join("\n"))?, format!("[{key}]"))
        };
        let (tokens, predicate) = predicates.iter().find(|(_, p)| super::predicates::without_conjunct(p, site).as_ref() != Some(p))?;
        let removal = if predicate.site() == site {
            let mut deletion = self.token_span(tokens.clone())?;
            let source = self.source.join("\n");
            while deletion.start > span.start && source.as_bytes().get(deletion.start - 1).is_some_and(u8::is_ascii_whitespace) { deletion.start -= 1; }
            deletion
        } else { self.conjunct_removal(predicate, site)? };
        let rewrite = self.source_rewrite(span.clone(), vec![(at..at, insertion), (removal, String::new())])?;
        let mut intended = read.clone();
        intended.filter = read.filter.as_deref().and_then(|p| super::predicates::without_conjunct(p, site)).map(Box::new);
        intended.keys.push(KeyPart { column: Some(column.into()), value: Expr::Member {
            base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: site.clone() }), name: column.into(), site: site.clone(),
        }, site: site.clone() });
        self.register_bytes(site, span, &rewrite, Some(intended))?;
        Some(rewrite)
    }

    fn conjunct_removal(&self, predicate: &Expr, site: &Site) -> Option<Range<usize>> {
        let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = predicate else { return None; };
        if op != "and" { return None; }
        let span = |expr: &Expr| self.token_span(self.expression_spans.get(&(expr.site().line, expr.site().col))?.clone());
        if left.site() == site { return Some(span(left)?.start..span(right)?.start); }
        if right.site() == site { return Some(span(left)?.end..span(right)?.end); }
        self.conjunct_removal(left, site).or_else(|| self.conjunct_removal(right, site))
    }

    pub(super) fn register_repair(&mut self, site: &Site, tokens: Range<usize>, rewrite: &str, intended: Option<ReadExpr>) {
        if let Some(span) = self.token_span(tokens) { self.register_bytes(site, span, rewrite, intended); }
    }

    pub(super) fn repair_message(&mut self, site: &Site, rewrite: &str, message: String) {
        if let Some(candidate) = self.document.source.repairs.iter_mut().rev()
            .find(|candidate| &candidate.site == site && candidate.rewrite == rewrite) {
            candidate.message = Some(message);
        }
    }

    fn register_bytes(&mut self, site: &Site, span: Range<usize>, rewrite: &str, intended: Option<ReadExpr>) -> Option<()> {
        if rewrite.is_empty() { return None; }
        let tokens = self.declaration_tokens()?;
        let declaration_site = self.tokens.get(tokens.start)?.site.clone();
        let start = self.source_offset(&declaration_site)?;
        let last = self.tokens.get(tokens.end)?;
        let source = self.source.join("\n");
        let end = if last.kind == Kind::Eof { source.len() } else { self.source_offset(&last.site)? };
        if span.end > end { return None; }
        self.document.source.repairs.push(crate::repair::Candidate {

    pub(super) fn scan_filter(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl) -> Fallible<(ReadExpr, Option<Box<Expr>>)> {
        let mut read = read.clone();
        let Some(mut predicate) = read.filter.take() else { return Ok((read, None)); };
        // Planning knows parameter declarations; binding also knows helper,
        // local and lane selectors. Only present, infallible endpoints may
        // leave the short-circuit predicate in either pass.
        let required = self.scopes.iter().flat_map(|scope| scope.keys())
            .filter(|name| self.binding(name).is_some_and(|binding| !binding.ty.is_optional()))
            .cloned().collect();
        if !read.order_keys.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none() {
            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
            let mut parts = Vec::new(); super::super::intent::split(*predicate, &mut parts);
            let mut residual = Vec::new();
            for part in parts {
                let candidate = super::super::intent::comparison(&part).map(|(column, op, value)| (column, vec![op], value))
                    .or_else(|| super::super::intent::equality(&part, info)
                        .filter(|(column, _)| !self.key_ty(info, index, column).is_optional())
                        .map(|(column, value)| (column, vec![">=".into(), "<=".into()], value)));
                if let Some((column, ops, value)) = candidate
                    && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(&column)
                    && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(&value, &required)
                    && ops.iter().all(|op| !read.seeks.iter().any(|bound| bound.op.starts_with('<') == op.starts_with('<'))) {
                    for op in ops { read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: column.clone(), op, value: value.clone(), site: part.site().clone() }); }
                } else { residual.push(part); }
            }
            let Some(remaining) = super::super::intent::conjunction(residual) else { return Ok((read, None)); };
            predicate = remaining;
        }
        // A single next-column comparison is the same seek as its key spelling.
        if read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
            && let A::Binary { op, left, right, site } = predicate.as_ref()
            && matches!(op.as_str(), ">" | ">=" | "<" | "<=")
            && let A::Member { base, name, .. } = left.as_ref()
            && matches!(base.as_ref(), A::Focal { .. })
            && index.columns.get(read.keys.len()) == Some(name)
            && !has_focal(right)
            && super::super::intent_aggregates::safe_endpoint(right, &required)
        {
            read.seeks.push(ast::SeekBound { column: name.clone(), op: op.clone(), value: *right.clone(), site: site.clone() });
            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(site, "N_SEEK", "a `where` on the next index column is a seek; write it in the key", print::read_expr(&read)).guide("reads"));
            return Ok((read, None));
        }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#20.3 — an unscoped filter can spend the whole page meter on unrelated authors.
        if self.kind == ProgramKind::Query && read.keys.is_empty() && read.seeks.is_empty() && read.matching.is_none()
            && crate::bind::unique_bound::group_bound(self.schema, &read.table, &[]).is_none_or(|n| n > 128)
            && has_focal(&predicate) && !pins_leading_column(&predicate, index.columns.first().map(String::as_str))
        {
            let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
            let follows = follow_edge_filter(&predicate, info);
            let consequence = if follows {
                "pages come back short and incomplete when followed authors are sparse (`complete: false`)"
            } else { "sparse matches can exhaust the row meter and return short `complete: false` pages" };
            // @ref LLP 3000.036#12 — wave 2 (024): a `where` every row passes is the trivial repair.
            let rewrite = match always_true(&predicate, info) {
                Some(column) => format!("drop this `where`: .{column} is required, so every row passes it"),
                None if follows => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, or read a `view` (see `snapback4 guide feeds`)".to_owned(),
                None => "pin a column with `where .col = value`, choose `by .col`, read a `view` for a count or total, or drop a `where` every row passes".to_owned(),
            };
            self.notes.push(Diagnostic::new(predicate.site(), "N_SPARSE_FILTER",
                format!("{} by {}: this unscoped `where` pins no index prefix; {consequence}", read.table, index.name),
                rewrite).guide(if follows { "feeds" } else { "reads" }));
        }

impl Catalog {
    pub fn build(documents: Vec<Document>, diagnostics: &mut Vec<Diagnostic>) -> Self {
        let mut tables: BTreeMap<String, TableInfo> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut maintains = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut shapes: BTreeMap<String, ShapeDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut functions: BTreeMap<String, FunctionDecl> = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effects = BTreeMap::new();
        let mut effect_sites: BTreeMap<String, Site> = BTreeMap::new();
        for doc in &documents {
            for table in &doc.tables {
                if tables.contains_key(&table.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&table.site, format!("duplicate table {}", table.name), "rename one of the two tables"));
                    continue;
                }
                let mut columns = BTreeMap::new();
                let mut ok = true;
                for column in &table.columns {
                    if column.name == "id" {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("table {} gets id implicitly; remove the id column", table.name), "delete this line"));
                        ok = false;
                        continue;
                    }
                    if columns.insert(column.name.clone(), column.type_.clone()).is_some() {
                        diagnostics.push(unknown(&column.site, format!("duplicate column {}.{}", table.name, column.name), "rename one of the two columns"));
                        ok = false;
                    }
                    diagnostics.push(Diagnostic::new(&maintain.site, "E_QUARRY_RESERVED",
                        format!("global maintained scalar `{}` would shadow Quarry's contextual `{}` builtin", maintain.name, maintain.name),
                        format!("rename the view, for example `view total{}: ...`", maintain.name[..1].to_uppercase() + &maintain.name[1..])).guide("maintains"));
                    continue;
                }
                if tables.contains_key(&maintain.name) || maintains.contains_key(&maintain.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&maintain.site, format!("maintain {} collides with a table of the same name", maintain.name), "rename the aggregate view"));
                    continue;
                }
                maintains.insert(maintain.name.clone(), maintain);
            }
            for shape in &doc.shapes {
                if let Some(first) = shapes.get(&shape.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&shape.site, format!("duplicate shape {}; first declared at {}", shape.name, first.site), "rename one shape"));
                    continue;
                }
                shapes.insert(shape.name.clone(), shape.clone());
            }
            for function in &doc.functions {
                if let Some(first) = functions.get(&function.name) {
                    diagnostics.push(unknown(&function.site, format!("duplicate fn {}; first declared at {}", function.name, first.site), "rename one function"));
                    continue;

    // The range and order may follow `where`. Wait for the complete read before
    // proposing a key, and only move a top-level conjunct across focal scopes.
    pub(super) fn finish_read_repair(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, start: usize, predicates: &[(std::ops::Range<usize>, Expr)], enclosing: Option<&str>, diagnostic_start: usize, probe: bool) {
        let Some(enclosing) = enclosing else { return; };
        let Some(filter) = read.filter.as_deref() else { return; };
        let Some(comparison) = self_comparison(filter, &|c| self.column_type(&read.table, c).is_some_and(|t| t.optional)) else { return; };
        let Expr::Binary { left, .. } = comparison else { return; };
        let Some(column) = focal_column(left) else { return; };
        let Some(inner_type) = self.column_type(&read.table, column) else { return; };
        if self.column_type(enclosing, column).as_ref() != Some(&inner_type) { return; }
        // Views have projected fields but do not admit named-key reads.
        let Some(table) = self.table_decl(&read.table) else { return; };
        if read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.is_none() || key.column.as_deref() == Some(column)) { return; }
        if !is_conjunct(filter, comparison.site()) { return; }
        let has_key = |name: &str| name == column || read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_deref() == Some(name));
        if let Some(index) = &read.index {
            let Some(index) = table.indexes.iter().find(|i| &i.name == index) else { return; };
            let count = read.keys.len() + 1;
            if index.columns.len() < count || !index.columns[..count].iter().all(|name| has_key(name)) { return; }
        }
        let bounded = probe || read.range.is_some() || column == "id"
            || table.caps.iter().any(|cap| cap.by.iter().all(|name| has_key(name)))
            || table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.unique && index.columns.iter().all(|name| has_key(name)));
        if !bounded { return; }
        let Some(rewrite) = self.keyed_read_rewrite(read, start, predicates, comparison.site(), column) else { return; };
        let description = self.comparison_source(comparison.site()).unwrap_or_else(|| format!(".{column}"));
        self.repair_message(comparison.site(), &rewrite,
            format!("{} compares the filtered {} row's .{column} with itself; bind the enclosing row with a named read key", description, read.table));
        if let Some(d) = self.diagnostics[diagnostic_start..].iter_mut().find(|d| d.code == "E_QUARRY_SELF_EQUALITY" && &d.site == comparison.site()) {
            d.rewrite = rewrite;
        }
    }
}

fn is_conjunct(value: &Expr, site: &Site) -> bool {
    if value.site() == site { return true; }
    matches!(value, Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } if op == "and" && (is_conjunct(left, site) || is_conjunct(right, site)))
}

fn focal_column(value: &Expr) -> Option<&str> {
    match value {
        Expr::Member { base, name, .. } if matches!(base.as_ref(), Expr::Focal { .. }) => Some(name),
        _ => None,
    }
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — key operands keep the enclosing focal row; a filter introduces its own.
fn self_comparison<'a>(value: &'a Expr, optional: &impl Fn(&str) -> bool) -> Option<&'a Expr> {
    if let Expr::Binary { op, left, right, .. } = value
        && let Some(column) = focal_column(left) {
        let same = match (op.as_str(), right.as_ref()) {
            ("=" | "!=" | "<" | "<=" | ">" | ">=", right) => focal_column(right) == Some(column),
            ("in", Expr::Array { items, .. }) => items.iter().any(|item| focal_column(item) == Some(column)),
            _ => false,
        };
        // Ordered read predicates are false on null: inclusive reflexive
        // ordering is a useful presence test for an optional column.
//! Intent planning precedes schema binding. Every inferred index is an ordinary
//! index; the runtime, adoption and residency never distinguish its provenance.
use super::catalog::{Catalog, TableInfo};
use super::intent_walk as walk;
use crate::ast::*;
use crate::diag::{Diagnostic, Site};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Clone, Default)]
pub(super) struct Requests {
    indexes: BTreeMap<(String, String), IndexDecl>,
    errors: Vec<Diagnostic>,
    contexts: super::intent_aggregates::Contexts,
    /// Reads that are a merge's lanes (or planned as one): their order is
    /// the merge's shared order, so an equality on it stays a seek.
    merged: std::collections::BTreeSet<String>,
    lane: bool,
    pub(super) cap_repairs: BTreeMap<String, String>,
}

// @ref LLP 3000.013#2.1 — intrinsic id resolves every table-local tie.
// Normalize authored table reads before view expansion: a view's shared order
// must retain later keys because different branches can carry the same id.
pub(super) fn table_order(catalog: &mut Catalog) {
    let mut normalize = |value: &mut Expr| {
        if let Expr::Read(read) = value && read.matching.is_some() && read.index.is_some()
            && let Some(table) = catalog.tables.get(&read.table) {
            let original = read.clone();
            if super::intent_search::named_order(table, read) {
                let source = catalog.source.expression(&Expr::Read(original));
                catalog.read_origins.entry(read.site.to_string()).or_insert_with(|| source.clone());
                catalog.canonical_read_origins.entry(read.site.to_string()).or_insert(source);
            }
        }
        if let Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } = value
            && catalog.tables.contains_key(&read.table)
            && let Some(id) = read.order_keys.iter().position(|column| column == "id")
            && id + 1 < read.order_keys.len() {
            catalog.read_origins.entry(read.site.to_string()).or_insert_with(|| catalog.source.expression(&Expr::Read(read.clone())));
            catalog.canonical_read_origins.entry(read.site.to_string()).or_insert_with(|| if read.index.is_none() { crate::print::read_expr(read) } else { catalog.source.expression(&Expr::Read(read.clone())) });
            read.order_keys.truncate(id + 1);
        }
    };
    for doc in &mut catalog.documents {
        walk::document(doc, &mut normalize);
        for view in &mut doc.views {
            for branch in &mut view.branches {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut branch.predicate { walk::expression(predicate, &mut normalize); }
                walk::shape(&mut branch.fields, &mut normalize);
            }
        }
    }
}

impl Requests {
    /// Plan a merge lane: an equality on its order column stays a seek, so
    /// every lane keeps the merge's shared order.
    pub(super) fn plan_lane(&mut self, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, read: &mut ReadExpr) {
        let prior = std::mem::replace(&mut self.lane, true);
        self.read(tables, read, false);
        self.lane = prior;
    }
    pub(super) fn error_count(&self) -> usize { self.errors.len() }

    pub(super) fn branch_index<'a>(&'a self, table: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<&'a IndexDecl> {
        let name = read.index.as_ref()?;
        self.indexes.get(&(table.decl.name.clone(), name.clone()))
            .or_else(|| table.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name))
    }

    pub(super) fn branch_index_fits(&self, table: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr) -> bool {
        let Some(name) = &read.index else { return false };
        if let Some(index) = self.indexes.get(&(table.decl.name.clone(), name.clone())) {
            // A pending structural token is renamed after planning. Bound
            // the emitted label, including its column names and collision suffix.
            super::key_bound::plain_index_fits(table, index, &index_name(table, index))
        } else {
            table.indexes.iter().find(|index| &index.name == name)
                .is_some_and(|index| super::key_bound::plain_index_fits(table, index, &index.name))
        }
    }

    /// Physical components in the selected plain index, including a pending
    /// structural request before its token receives the emitted index name.
    pub(super) fn branch_index_width(&self, table: &TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr) -> Option<usize> {
        self.branch_index(table, read).filter(|index| index.is_plain()).map(|index| index.columns.len())
    }

    fn index(&mut self, table: &TableInfo, columns: Vec<String>, words: Option<(usize, Vec<String>)>, site: &Site, exact: bool) -> String {
        self.index_computed(table, columns, words, BTreeMap::new(), site, exact)
    }

    fn index_computed(&mut self, table: &TableInfo, columns: Vec<String>, words: Option<(usize, Vec<String>)>, computed: BTreeMap<String, Expr>, site: &Site, exact: bool) -> String {
        let computed_key = computed_descriptor(table, &computed);
        let mut matching = table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| computed_descriptor(table, &index.computed) == computed_key && index.words == words
            && if exact { index.columns == columns } else { index.columns.starts_with(&columns) }).collect::<Vec<_>>();
        matching.sort_by_key(|index| &index.name);
        if exact && matching.len() > 1 {
            self.errors.push(crate::diag::ambiguous(site, format!("{} has several declared indexes for the same planned read: {}", table.decl.name, matching.iter().map(|index| index.name.as_str()).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ")),
                format!("choose `by {}` explicitly or keep one definition", matching[0].name)));
        }
        if let Some(index) = matching.first() { return index.name.clone(); }
        // A structural request token is replaced only after all requests have
        // been sorted. Collisions therefore cannot depend on program order.
        let token = format!("$intent:{}", snapback4_core::value::digest(&(&columns, &words, &computed_key, false)));
        self.indexes.entry((table.decl.name.clone(), token.clone())).or_insert_with(|| IndexDecl {
            name: token.clone(), columns, words, computed, unique: false, refusal: None, site: site.clone(),
        });
        token
    }

    pub(super) fn read(&mut self, tables: &BTreeMap<String, TableInfo>, read: &mut ReadExpr, rule: bool) {
        if super::validate_key_names(read).is_err() { return; }
        let Some(table) = tables.get(&read.table) else { return; };
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#23 — resolve count-row aliases before choosing an index;
        // an authored group column named count always keeps precedence.
        for column in read.order_keys.iter_mut().chain(read.keys.iter_mut().filter_map(|key| key.column.as_mut()))
            .chain(read.seeks.iter_mut().map(|seek| &mut seek.column)) {
            *column = row_column(table, column).into();
        }
        if super::validate_key_names(read).is_err() { return; }
        // @ref LLP 3000.007#18.1 — normalized named search orders keep their
        // explicit choice for a compatible browse plan; words use normal planning.
        let preferred = read.index.as_ref().filter(|name| !read.order_keys.is_empty()
            && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| &index.name == *name && index.unique && index.is_plain())).cloned();
        if preferred.is_some() { read.index = None; }
        if read.index.is_some() {
            if !rule { self.selected_prefix(table, read); }
            return;
        }
        let named = !read.keys.is_empty() && read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        // @ref LLP 3000.013#2.2 — exact named unique keys select points;
        // binding requires non-null arguments on nullable key columns.
        let covered = named && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.unique && index.is_plain()
            && (index.columns.len() == read.keys.len() || table.total_unique(index))
            && index.columns.iter().all(|column| read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column))));
        if rule {
            if named && !covered {
                read.index = Some(self.index(table, read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone()).collect(), None, &read.site, true));
            }
            return;
        }
        if (covered || table.maintained.is_some() && named && table.indexes.iter().any(|index| index.unique && index.columns.iter().all(|column| read.keys.iter().any(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column))))) && read.range.is_none() && read.filter.is_none() && read.matching.is_none() && read.order_keys.is_empty() { return; }
        // Positional keys retain the binder's point/refusal path. They are never
        // silently turned into a predicate on an inferred parent column.
        if !read.keys.is_empty() && !named { return; }
        let mut pins: BTreeMap<String, KeyPart> = read.keys.iter().filter_map(|key| key.column.clone().map(|name| (name, key.clone()))).collect();
        let mut residual = Vec::new();
        if let Some(predicate) = read.filter.take() {
            let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
            split(*predicate, &mut conjuncts);
            for predicate in conjuncts {
                if let Some((column, value)) = equality(&predicate, table) {
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036#14.1 — a page that pins its first order column
                    // (`where .tester = viewer … by .tester`) reads that prefix: its rule is
                    // decided by the pin, as `by .id` already was. A merge lane or an
                    // extremum keeps the seek, so every lane shares one order; so does a view
                    // (a ranking pinned by its value is refused as it was).
                    let site = read.site.to_string();
                    let own_order = !self.lane && table.maintained.is_none() && !self.merged.contains(&site) && !self.contexts.extrema.contains_key(&site);
                    if own_order && read.order_keys.first() == Some(&column) && column != "id" && read.matching.is_none() && !pins.contains_key(&column) {
                        pins.insert(column.clone(), KeyPart { column: Some(column), value, site: predicate.site().clone() });
                        continue;
                    }
                    // Keep the full ordered suffix in every projected branch,
                    // even when the predicate fixes one of its order fields.
                    // A membership lane may pin the target row's intrinsic id
                    // while retaining the page's logical `.order, id`
                    // position. Planning `[id, order]` leaves one exact row;
                    // the binder/runtime still compare its projected logical
                    // position with every other lane.
                    // @ref LLP 3000.036.001#15 — row-id target lanes pin id without dropping the shared merge position.
                    if (read.order_keys.contains(&column) || column == "id") && !(self.lane && column == "id") {
                        if read.order_keys.first().map(String::as_str).unwrap_or("id") == column
                            && !table.columns.get(&column).is_some_and(|ty| ty.optional)
                            && self.contexts.safe(read, &value) {
                            for op in [">=", "<="] {
                                residual.push(Expr::Binary { op: op.into(), left: Box::new(Expr::Member { base: Box::new(Expr::Focal { site: predicate.site().clone() }), name: column.clone(), site: predicate.site().clone() }), right: Box::new(value.clone()), site: predicate.site().clone() });
                            }
                        } else { residual.push(predicate); }
                        continue;
                    }
                    if !pins.contains_key(&column) {
                        pins.insert(column.clone(), KeyPart { column: Some(column), value, site: predicate.site().clone() });
                        continue;
                    }
                }
                residual.push(predicate);
            }
        }
        let mut columns: Vec<_> = pins.keys().filter(|column| !read.computed.contains_key(*column)).cloned().collect();
        columns.extend(pins.keys().filter(|column| read.computed.contains_key(*column)).cloned());
        let mut order = read.order_keys.clone();
        if order.is_empty() { order.push("id".into()); }
        // A projected field may alias id before another order key. Moving it
        // changes the lane's order relative to the view's shared cursor.
        let mut seen = std::collections::BTreeSet::new();
        order.retain(|column| !pins.contains_key(column) && seen.insert(column.clone()));
        if !order.iter().any(|column| column == "id") { order.push("id".into()); }
        let words = if read.matching.is_some() {
            let fields = if !table.decl.search.is_empty() { table.decl.search.clone() }
                else {
                    let definitions: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = table.indexes.iter().filter_map(|index| index.words.as_ref().map(|(_, fields)| fields.clone())).collect();
                    if definitions.len() > 1 {
                        self.errors.push(crate::diag::ambiguous(&read.site, format!("{} has multiple words definitions", read.table),
                            format!("declare `search {}` on {} or choose an authored words index explicitly", definitions.first().unwrap().join(", "), read.table)).guide("search"));
                    }
                    definitions.into_iter().next().unwrap_or_default()
                };
            if fields.is_empty() { read.filter = conjunction(residual); return; }
            Some((columns.len(), fields))
        } else { None };
        // Seek extraction leaves duplicate bounds as residuals; the strongest
        // range is still exact after filtering and no conjunct disappears.
        let mut kept = Vec::new();
        for predicate in residual {
            if words.is_none() && let Some((column, op, value)) = comparison(&predicate)
                .map(|(column, op, value)| (row_column(table, &column).to_owned(), op, value))
                && order.first() == Some(&column) && self.contexts.safe(read, &value)
                && !read.seeks.iter().any(|bound| (bound.op.starts_with('<')) == op.starts_with('<')) {
                read.seeks.push(SeekBound { column, op, value, site: predicate.site().clone() });
            } else {
                kept.push(predicate);
            }
        }
        // An extremum has no observable tie order. Reuse an endpoint index
        // even when its equality prefix permutes the canonical pin order or
        // its suffix carries additional tie columns. Ordinary pages keep the
        // exact-order planner below.
        let extremum_index = self.contexts.extrema.get(&read.site.to_string())
            .filter(|field| words.is_none() && order.first() == Some(field))
            .and_then(|field| table.indexes.iter().filter(|index| index.is_plain()
                && index.columns.get(columns.len()) == Some(field)
                && index.columns[..columns.len()].iter().all(|column| columns.contains(column)))
                .min_by_key(|index| &index.name));
        if let Some(index) = extremum_index { columns = index.columns[..columns.len()].to_vec(); }
        read.keys = columns.iter().filter_map(|column| pins.remove(column)).collect();
        read.filter = conjunction(kept);
        read.order_keys = order.clone();

#![forbid(unsafe_code)]
//! Quarry for Snapback 4: one language for tables, rules and programs
//! (LLP 2000.000 §5.1), bound to the core IR in Rust so `check` runs in
//! milliseconds with no other runtime.

pub mod ast;
pub mod bind;
pub mod diag;
pub mod emit;
pub mod guide;
pub mod lexer;
pub mod parser;
pub mod print;
pub mod source;
pub mod recipe;
mod repair;
pub mod types;
pub mod release_changes;

pub use bind::{Bound, BoundProgram, bind};
pub use diag::{Diagnostic, Site};
pub use parser::parse_document;

/// Parse and bind a set of `(file name, source)` documents.
pub fn compile(sources: &[(String, String)]) -> Bound {
    let mut documents = Vec::new();
    let mut diagnostics = Vec::new();
    for (file, source) in sources {
        let (document, mut parsed) = parse_document(file, source);
        diagnostics.append(&mut parsed);
        documents.push(document);
    }
    // Refusals still block binding. On these malformed forms only, complete
    // their repairs with column types/table names from the whole project,
    // including files and declarations that follow the offending operation.
    let repair_files: std::collections::BTreeSet<_> = diagnostics.iter().filter(|d|
        d.code == "E_QUARRY_SELF_EQUALITY" || (d.code == "E_QUARRY_UNSUPPORTED" && d.message.starts_with("count(*)")))
        .map(|d| d.site.file.clone()).collect();
    if !repair_files.is_empty() {
        let tables: Vec<_> = documents.iter().flat_map(|doc| doc.tables.iter().cloned()).collect();
        // Use the ordinary binder's projected types, including renamed and
        // computed fields. Invalid or non-scalar projections get no invented
        // argument type. This discarded binding is diagnostic-only.
        let views = if documents.iter().any(|doc| !doc.views.is_empty() || !doc.feed_views.is_empty()) {
            let mut declarations = documents.clone();
            for doc in &mut declarations {
                doc.programs.clear(); doc.actions.clear(); doc.routes.clear(); doc.schedules.clear();
            }
            bind(declarations).view_types
        } else { Default::default() };
        diagnostics.retain(|d| !repair_files.contains(&d.site.file));
        for ((file, source), document) in sources.iter().zip(&mut documents) {
            if repair_files.contains(file) {
                let (repaired, mut parsed) = parser::parse_with_types(file, source, tables.clone(), views.clone());
                *document = repaired;
                diagnostics.append(&mut parsed);
            }
        }
    }
    bind::audience_diagnostics::parser_context(&documents, &mut diagnostics);
    repair::verify(&documents, &mut diagnostics);
    if !diagnostics.is_empty() {
        return Bound { receipt_members: Default::default(), refusal_sites: Default::default(), view_types: Default::default(), server: documents.iter().any(|doc| doc.server), schema: Default::default(), programs: Vec::new(), personas: Vec::new(), diagnostics, notes: Vec::new() };
    }
    bind(documents)
}

    // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.2 — an explicit index has the same coverage as
    // its bracket prefix, but only for present, infallible binding values.
    fn selected_prefix(&self, table: &TableInfo, read: &mut ReadExpr) {
        let Some(index) = table.indexes.iter().find(|index| Some(&index.name) == read.index.as_ref()) else { return; };
        if !index.is_plain() || read.keys.len() > index.columns.len() || read.range.is_none()
            || read.matching.is_some() || !read.order_keys.is_empty() || !read.seeks.is_empty() { return; }
        // Preserve positional keys and reject gaps/mismatches through the
        // ordinary binder. Never repair an invalid authored constraint here.
        if read.keys.iter().enumerate().any(|(n, key)| key.column.as_ref().is_some_and(|column| column != &index.columns[n])) { return; }
        let Some(predicate) = read.filter.as_ref() else { return; };
        let original_prefix = read.keys.len();
        let mut conjuncts = Vec::new();
        split(*predicate.clone(), &mut conjuncts);
        for column in &index.columns[read.keys.len()..] {
            // A range needs an unpinned component. Existing seeks take the
            // unchanged binder path above, including originally invalid seeks.
            if read.keys.len() + 1 == index.columns.len() { break; }
            if !table.columns.get(column).is_some_and(|ty| !ty.optional) { break; }
            let candidates: Vec<_> = conjuncts.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(n, predicate)| {
                if !matches!(predicate, Expr::Binary { op, .. } if op == "=") { return None; }
                equality(predicate, table).filter(|(name, _)| name == column).map(|(_, value)| (n, value))
            }).collect();
            // Multiple equalities may conflict. Leave all of them residual;
            // no arbitrary choice may acquire a different coverage domain.
            let [(n, value)] = candidates.as_slice() else { break; };
            // Only the leading conjunct may move: skipping an earlier row
            // predicate could suppress its explicit refusal or arithmetic error.
            if *n != 0 || !self.contexts.safe(read, value) { break; }
            let predicate = conjuncts.remove(*n);
            // Existing positional prefixes must stay positional, too.
            let named = read.keys.first().is_none_or(|key| key.column.is_some());
            read.keys.push(KeyPart { column: named.then(|| column.clone()), value: value.clone(), site: predicate.site().clone() });
        }
        // An unchanged predicate keeps its original source span, which is
        // also the input to source-preserving diagnostic repairs.
        if read.keys.len() != original_prefix {
            if read.range.as_ref().is_some_and(|range| range.cursor.is_some()) {
                read.cursor_prefix.get_or_insert(original_prefix);
            }
            read.filter = conjunction(conjuncts);
        }
    }
}

//! Mutable syntax traversal shared by planning and final index-name resolution.
use crate::ast::*;

pub(crate) fn expression(value: &mut Expr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    match value {
        Expr::Read(read) | Expr::Exists { read, .. } | Expr::Created { read, .. } => read_children(read, visit),
        Expr::Unary { value, .. } | Expr::Member { base: value, .. } | Expr::Assert { value, .. } | Expr::ShapeUse { base: value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
        Expr::Binary { left, right, .. } => { expression(left, visit); expression(right, visit); }
        Expr::Array { items, .. } | Expr::Call { args: items, .. } => { for item in items { expression(item, visit); } }
        Expr::Object { fields, .. } => { for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } } }
        Expr::Shape { base, fields, .. } => { expression(base, visit); shape(fields, visit); }
        Expr::If { then, condition, otherwise, .. } => { expression(then, visit); expression(condition, visit); expression(otherwise, visit); }
        Expr::Walk { seed, .. } => expression(seed, visit),
        Expr::Write { keys, fields, condition, .. } => {
            for key in keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
            for field in fields { if let Some(value) = &mut field.value { expression(value, visit); } }
            if let Some(value) = condition { expression(value, visit); }
        }
        Expr::Merge(merge) => {
            for value in [&mut merge.matching, &mut merge.filter, &mut merge.cursor].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
            let mut lanes = Vec::new();
            for mut lane in std::mem::take(&mut merge.lanes) {
                if let Some(predicate) = &mut lane.admission { expression(predicate, visit); }
                if let Some((_, read)) = &mut lane.nulls {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    let Expr::Read(planned) = value else { unreachable!("planned null scan") };
                    **read = planned;
                }
                if let Some(value) = &mut lane.distinct { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(fields) = &mut lane.project { shape(fields, visit); }
                for value in &mut lane.prefix { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some((_, value)) = &mut lane.loop_ { expression(value, visit); }
                if let Some(read) = &mut lane.planned {
                    let mut value = Expr::Read(*read.clone());
                    expression(&mut value, visit);
                    match value {
                        Expr::Read(planned) => **read = planned,
                        Expr::Merge(nested) if lane.loop_.is_none() => {
                            for mut child in nested.lanes {
                                child.branch = lane.branch.clone();
                                child.project = lane.project.clone();
                                lanes.push(child);
                            }
                            continue;
                        }
                        _ => unreachable!("a planned lane is a read or an expanded read"),
                    }
                }
                lanes.push(lane);
            }
            merge.lanes = lanes;
        }
        Expr::Name { .. } | Expr::Focal { .. } | Expr::Literal { .. } => {}
    }
    visit(value);
}

fn read_children(read: &mut ReadExpr, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for key in &mut read.keys { expression(&mut key.value, visit); }
    for seek in &mut read.seeks { expression(&mut seek.value, visit); }
    for value in [&mut read.filter, &mut read.matching].into_iter().flatten() { expression(value, visit); }
    if let Some(value) = read.range.as_mut().and_then(|range| range.cursor.as_mut()) { expression(value, visit); }
}

pub(super) fn shape(fields: &mut [ShapeField], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for field in fields { if let ShapeField::Field { value, .. } = field { expression(value, visit); } }
}

pub(super) fn statements(body: &mut [Stmt], visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for stmt in body {
        match stmt {
            Stmt::Bind { value, .. } | Stmt::Expr { value, .. } | Stmt::Return { value, .. } => expression(value, visit),
            Stmt::Require { condition, .. } => expression(condition, visit),
            Stmt::Emit { payload, once, .. } => {
                expression(payload, visit);
                if let Some(key) = once { expression(key, visit); }
            }
            Stmt::If { condition, then_body, else_body, .. } => { expression(condition, visit); statements(then_body, visit); statements(else_body, visit); }
            Stmt::As { principal: domain, body, .. } | Stmt::For { domain, body, .. } => { expression(domain, visit); statements(body, visit); }
            Stmt::Break { .. } | Stmt::Continue { .. } => {}
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) fn document(doc: &mut Document, visit: &mut impl FnMut(&mut Expr)) {
    for program in &mut doc.programs { statements(&mut program.prelude, visit); statements(&mut program.body, visit); }
    for function in &mut doc.functions {
        match &mut function.body { FunctionBody::Expression(value) => expression(value, visit), FunctionBody::Block(body) => statements(body, visit) }
    }
    for decl in &mut doc.shapes { shape(&mut decl.fields, visit); }
}

//! Seeks and row-local scan predicates.
use super::*;

impl<'a> Binder<'a> {
    // @ref LLP 3000.011.000.001#4 — retain the binder's actual choice for why, never infer a new lookup or rewrite.
    pub(super) fn record_selection(&mut self, site: &str, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, result: &str, reason: &str) {
        let named = read.keys.iter().all(|key| key.column.is_some());
        let bindings = index.columns.iter().enumerate().filter_map(|(position, column)| {
            let key = if named { read.keys.iter().find(|key| key.column.as_ref() == Some(column)) } else { read.keys.get(position) }?;
            Some(format!("{column} = {}", self.catalog.source.operand(&key.value)))
        }).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(", ");
        let origin = if self.catalog.synthesized.contains(&(read.table.clone(), index.name.clone())) { "synthesized; " } else { "" };
        let source = if origin.is_empty() { String::new() } else { format!("; read: {}", self.catalog.read_origins.get(&read.site.to_string()).cloned().unwrap_or_else(|| print::read_expr(read))) };
        let mut selection = format!("selected {} ({bindings}); result: {result}; {origin}{reason}{source}", index.name);
        if !self.variants.is_empty() {
            let when = self.variants.iter().map(|(name, present)| format!("{} {}", name.trim_start_matches("$intentPresent:").trim_start_matches("$intentSearch:"), if *present { "present" } else { "absent" })).collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" and ");
            selection = format!("when {when}: {selection}");
        }
        self.selections.entry(site.into()).and_modify(|prior| { if !prior.contains(&selection) { prior.push_str("; "); prior.push_str(&selection); } }).or_insert(selection);
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#10 — a covered total unique key reads one row; additional named equalities check that row.
    pub(super) fn read_value(&mut self, read: &ReadExpr, mode: ReadMode, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Expr> {
        let info = self.table(&read.table, &read.site)?;
        for key in &read.keys { if let Some(field) = &key.column { super::super::row_views::predicate_field(info, field, &read.site)?; } }
        if !read.seeks.is_empty() { return Err(type_error(&read.site, "a seek needs a bounded page", "t[prefix, col >= start] first 50 by index")); }
        let mut point = read.clone();
        if point.keys.is_empty() && info.maintained.as_ref().is_some_and(|maintain| maintain.by.is_empty()) {
            point.keys.push(ast::KeyPart { column: None, value: A::Literal { value: Literal::Bool(true), site: read.site.clone() }, site: read.site.clone() });
            point.index = Some("byGroup".into());
        } else if point.keys.is_empty() {
            return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{}[] reads nothing; a point read needs a key", read.table), format!("{}[id]", read.table)));
        }
        let implicit_group = mode == ReadMode::FilteredPoint && info.maintained.is_some() && point.index.is_none();
        if implicit_group { point.index = Some("byGroup".into()); }
        let (mut keys, index, mut selection) = self.select_index(info, &point, mode, out)?;
        super::super::point_key::check(info, &index, read, &keys.iter().map(|(column, _, ty)|
            (column.as_deref(), !ty.is_optional() && *ty != Ty::Unknown)).collect::<Vec<_>>(), false)?;
        if implicit_group { selection = "complete maintained group key".into(); }
        let extra = keys.len() > index.columns.len();
        let mut equalities = Vec::new();
        if extra {
            for (column, value, ty) in &mut keys {
                let column = column.as_ref().expect("named key");
                if column != "id" && !info.columns.contains_key(column) {
                    return Err(unknown(&read.site, format!("{} has no column {column}", read.table), name_rewrite(column, info.columns.keys().map(String::as_str).chain(["id"]), None)));
                }
                let expected = self.key_ty(info, &index, column);
                if !ty.comparable(&expected) { return Err(type_error(&read.site, format!("{column} is {}; {}.{column} is {}", ty.name(), read.table, expected.name()), format!("use {} for {column}", expected.name()))); }
                *value = self.snapshot(value.clone(), out);
                if !index.columns.contains(column) { equalities.push((column.clone(), value.clone())); }
            }
            keys.retain(|(column, _, _)| index.columns.contains(column.as_ref().unwrap()));
        }
        let key = self.order_keys(info, &index, keys)?;
        let location = self.site();
        self.record_selection(&location, read, &index, if mode == ReadMode::Exists { "exists" } else if info.maintained.is_some() { "scalar" } else { "row or null" }, &selection);
        self.sources.insert(location.clone(), (read.site.clone(), print::read_expr(read)));
        let mut point = Read { site: location, table: read.table.clone(), index: index.name, key, provenance: KeyProvenance::Shape };
        // @ref LLP 3000.028#3.4 — only a caller-proved existence read may use the whole owner partition.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && equalities.is_empty() {
            super::super::own_keys::existence(self.schema, &mut point);
        }
        if equalities.is_empty() { return Ok(if mode == ReadMode::Exists { Expr::Exists(point) } else { Expr::Get(point) }); }
        let row = self.temp("probe");
        out.push(Stmt::Let { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Get(point) });
        let mut condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::var(&row)), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
        // @ref LLP 3000.014#5-what-derives-from-the-capture — a denied aggregate carries a null value, not a present group.
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists && info.maintained.is_some() {
            let present = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Ne, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), "value")), right: Box::new(Expr::Literal(Value::Null)) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(present) };
        }
        for (column, value) in equalities {
            let test = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::Eq, left: Box::new(ir::field(ir::var(&row), &column)), right: Box::new(value) };
            condition = Expr::Binary { op: BinaryOp::And, left: Box::new(condition), right: Box::new(test) };
        }
        if mode == ReadMode::Exists { return Ok(condition); }
        out.push(Stmt::If { condition, then_body: Vec::new(), else_body: vec![Stmt::Assign { name: row.clone(), value: Expr::Literal(Value::Null) }] });
        Ok(ir::var(&row))
    }

    // @ref LLP 3000.007#5.3 — a seek constrains the first index column after the pinned prefix.
    pub(super) fn seek(&mut self, info: &'a TableInfo, read: &ReadExpr, index: &ast::IndexDecl, out: &mut setup::Body) -> Fallible<Option<ir::Range>> {
        if read.seeks.is_empty() { return Ok(None); }

//! Compiled examples first; diagnostic details are under `why`.
pub(super) const CARD: &str = r##"# Reads

Read authorized rows. `sync … last N` bounds acquisition; cursors page server history (offline: acquired rows).

```quarry
query readForms(roomId: rooms, messageId: messages, c: cursor ?):
  one = messages[messageId]
  must = messages[messageId] ! else NOT_FOUND
  roster = members where .roomId = roomId
  member = exists members[roomId, viewer]
  admin = exists members[roomId = roomId, principal = viewer, role = 'admin']
  return { one, must, roster, member, admin }
query newest(roomId: rooms, c: cursor ?):
  return messages where .roomId = roomId and .body != null last 20 by .at after c
query recentTotal(roomId: rooms, start: time, end: time):
  return count(messages where .roomId = roomId and .at >= start and .at <= end)
```

`one` returns a row/null; `must` refuses missing/hidden rows.
`roster` reads the capped room; `member`/`admin` test membership.
`newest` pages newest-first; `recentTotal` counts under the meter.
Repeated `where` clauses combine with `and` in order.

## Limits

- A positional key names one row; use `where` for populations: E_QUARRY_POSITIONAL_SCAN.
- Add a cursor if the screen pages: N_NO_CURSOR.
- Unmerged pages with unpinned rule columns cannot certify all callers. N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE names a readable pinned-parent guard; N_NEVER_COMPLETE has none (separate grants may still admit callers).
- A nested read with ambiguous modifiers needs parentheses: E_QUARRY_MODIFIER_SCOPE.
- N_SHAPE_METER: maintained totals (guide views), previews, or queries.

## Details

Unreadable query references act absent: null points or complete empty pages. Mutation references refuse; ids grant no authority.

Reads select authorized rows by id, predicate or search text.
Add an order, page size and cursor to browse a population.

Projections drop `id` unless listed: `{ title }` omits id; `{ id, title }` preserves it. Whole rows retain id.


### Pages and cursors

State the rows, then the order and page. `messages where .roomId = roomId
last 20 by .at after c` means the newest matching messages. The compiler
chooses the index. Positional keys select identity for `id`/same-table references,
otherwise the declared unique key accepting types in order; ambiguous types need named columns.
The rules run before any projection, count or order reaches the caller.
Declare `c: cursor ?`, read `after c`, pass each answer's `next` as `c`; `usePage` does that.

### Completeness

Pinned-parent guards are caller-dependent (`N_CONDITIONAL_COMPLETE`; `guide recipes`).
`T[]` requests a complete fitting group: `return children[] where .parent = parent`.
It needs authority and a cap/unique bound. Update capped cases that now answer complete.

`complete` means the query answered its declared request within known coverage and execution limits.
Filling a declared page bound is complete.

Every ready `Read` carries `cut: 'meter' | 'words' | null` beside `complete`,
on the server and either device store. An empty page with `cut: 'meter'`
means partial: retry narrower, or continue if `next` exists; never "no matches".
`words` means the first term expanded past 32 words in an admitted non-uniform scope: narrow that
term or use scoped search (guide search). If both limits cut, `meter` wins.
Device reads and uniform server reads keep precise reasons.
Non-uniform server reads report `cut: null`: the reason is withheld, with `complete: false` still truthful.
`null` means "not disclosed here", not "not cut"; it also covers a horizon,
missing coverage. For `complete: false` with
`cut: null`, render "Results may be incomplete", even when empty; never "no matches".
A complete empty page has `cut: null` and `complete: true`.
Same-key observations retain their last answer marked `revalidating` during
plain refetch, reconnect and transport failure. Omitted `keep` and `keep: true`
share this policy; `keep: false` withdraws immediately. Inserts and non-policy updates retain through retries; deletes, policy-input changes and unknown evidence start a nonrenewable one-second bound. Bounded failure/timeout withdraws; replacements can still publish.
A notified cut keeps older replica answers ineligible until sync publishes it.
A whole server answer at that cut may replace sooner. Expiry, sign-out,
credential, store, schema and generation resets still withdraw immediately.
`fresh` and `since` describe server freshness, independently of revalidation.
With `keep: true`, a watch also lingers for 30 seconds after its last subscriber
leaves, sharing the same observation, expiry, invalidation and reset fences.
Local commits still revalidate it while unmounted; account/backend changes,
`clear_rows`, `settle_exit`, ephemeral resets and close end its authority.
See `guide client` for the complete retention and screen-lifetime recipe.
A ready list read always carries an array; an absent point can still be null.
`usePage` returns `items: []` while its first page is loading; earlier pages
remain visible while a later page is unavailable.

For device reads, online fallback asks only for missing facts or unknown presence.
Meter/word cuts and cursorless pages stay local, as do held queries and retained left groups.
Predictable pending writes block server fallback for dependent reads; unknown prediction
footprints block every query. Unrelated writes do not block. Withheld speculation
(past-horizon totals, extrema or points) stays `loading`; local cuts stay partial.
For one-shots, see `guide client`.
Keep a screen query locally complete (`snapback4 why <query>`); put server-needing
decorations in their own query so the main list never waits on them.
Keep the screen shell and draft mounted; render current reads.

For an expiring table, return the deadline with each row:

```quarry
query expiringMessages(roomId: rooms, c: cursor ?):
  return messages where .roomId = roomId after c last 20 by .at { id, body, expiresAt }
```

Held device observations republish at `nextExpiry`, including offline; render
those rows directly. Timers follow the row's deadline; a suspended page or a
busy host can delay the callback.
Only server-answered reads need a render-deadline filter to hide a returned
row while its next network answer is pending. Return `expiresAt`, as above.
In React, `useNow(deadline)` owns the timer and responds to clock estimates:

```ts
import { useClient, useNow } from "snapback4/react";
function useExpiringRows<T extends { expiresAt: number | null }>(rows: T[]) {
  const client = useClient();
  const deadline = Math.min(...rows.flatMap(row => row.expiresAt !== null && row.expiresAt > client.now() ? [row.expiresAt] : []));
  const now = useNow(Number.isFinite(deadline) ? deadline : null);
  return rows.filter(row => row.expiresAt === null || row.expiresAt > now);
}
```

Render the rows returned by `useExpiringRows(rows)`. `client.now()` estimates the server's time and never
moves backward; `null` never expires. The hook cleans up on unmount.

For typing and viewing indicators, use an ephemeral table (`guide ephemeral`,
also `guide typing`). Keep its server query separate from the device-held
chats list. The expiring-rows recipe above is for durable disappearing messages.

## Measure live propagation

Mounted reads wake from `/changes`, sync and re-evaluate affected queries, including parent and authorization dependencies.
Measure two signed-in clients with queries mounted: record sender invocation, `sent` acknowledgement, changed `observe` snapshot, React `useLayoutEffect` and image `onLoad` separately.
Acknowledgement follows commit; `waitSeconds: 0` measures custody. Inspect `/changes`, `/sync` and query requests in Network to separate transport from application work.
Measure media separately; repeat 20+ times, idle and loaded.

## Nullable predicates and enum lanes

For a required enum with at most eight variants, `.status != 'done'` opens
the same pinned value lanes as `.status in ['todo', 'doing']`. A bound value
can also select the excluded variant. Fallible expressions remain residual
predicates so their guards still run first. Nullable enums retain a residual
inequality, including null rows; larger enums also stay residual.
Value lanes are chosen only when every lane's prefix decides the source read
rule. Otherwise both `!=` and `in` remain residual filters on one scan, which
can continue through visible rows with `next`; the non-uniform read still
reports `complete: false`.

```quarry
table workTasks:
  owner: principal
  cap 100 by owner
  due: time ?
  status: enum('todo', 'doing', 'done')
  assignee: principal ?
  createdAt: time
  read <- .owner = viewer
  sync to .owner
shape workTaskCard = { id, due }
query overdueTasks(c: cursor ?):
  return workTasks where .owner = viewer and .due < now first 50 by .due after c
